Method: 1. 学习每个运动员战术击球的生成模型；2. 将运动员特征嵌入共同潜在空间，编码个体特征（包括技能水平）；3. 使用大规模3D重建专业比赛数据集训练；4. 考虑全面的游戏情境（运动员位置、对手行为）；5. 训练简单的相对排名网络进行技能预测。

Result: 学习到的运动员空间反映了不同的比赛风格和属性，这些属性共同代表了技能水平。通过相对排名网络，既能实现相对技能预测，也能实现绝对技能预测。结果表明该空间能有效量化技能水平。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂交互行为中的自动化技能评估提供了基础，通过学习运动员战术身份和游戏情境，能够在潜在空间中有效编码和量化技能水平。

Abstract: Gauging an individual's skill level is crucial, as it inherently shapes their behavior. Quantifying skill, however, is challenging because it is latent to the observed actions. To explore skill understanding in human behavior, we focus on dyadic sports -- specifically table tennis -- where skill manifests not just in complex movements, but in the subtle nuances of execution conditioned on game context. Our key idea is to learn a generative model of each player's tactical racket strokes and jointly embed them in a common latent space that encodes individual characteristics, including those pertaining to skill levels. By training these player models on a large-scale dataset of 3D-reconstructed professional matches and conditioning them on comprehensive game context -- including player positioning and opponent behaviors -- the models capture individual tactical identities within their latent space. We probe this learned player space and find that it reflects distinct play styles and attributes that collectively represent skill. By training a simple relative ranking network on these embeddings, we demonstrate that both relative and absolute skill predictions can be achieved. These results demonstrate that the learned player space effectively quantifies skill levels, providing a foundation for automated skill assessment in complex, interactive behaviors.

</details>


### [151] [PSDesigner: Automated Graphic Design with a Human-Like Creative Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25738)
*Xincheng Shuai,Song Tang,Yutong Huang,Henghui Ding,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PSDesigner是一个模仿人类设计师工作流程的自动化图形设计系统，通过专业组件收集主题相关素材并自主推断执行工具调用来操作设计文件，基于CreativePSD数据集训练模型学习专家设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化设计系统通常简化专业工作流程，导致灵活性和直观性有限，无法忠实将用户意图转换为可编辑的设计文件，需要开发更符合人类设计师工作方式的系统。

Method: 构建PSDesigner系统，包含多个专业组件：基于用户指令收集主题相关素材，自主推断和执行工具调用来操作PSD设计文件（如整合新素材或优化劣质元素）。创建CreativePSD数据集，包含大量高质量PSD文件及操作轨迹标注，涵盖广泛设计场景和艺术风格，用于训练模型学习专家设计流程。

Result: 大量实验表明，PSDesigner在多样化图形设计任务中优于现有方法，使非专业人士能够方便地创建生产质量的设计作品。

Conclusion: PSDesigner通过模仿人类设计师工作流程，解决了现有自动化设计系统的局限性，提供了更灵活直观的设计体验，使非专业人士也能创作专业质量的设计作品。

Abstract: Graphic design is a creative and innovative process that plays a crucial role in applications such as e-commerce and advertising. However, developing an automated design system that can faithfully translate user intentions into editable design files remains an open challenge. Although recent studies have leveraged powerful text-to-image models and MLLMs to assist graphic design, they typically simplify professional workflows, resulting in limited flexibility and intuitiveness. To address these limitations, we propose PSDesigner, an automated graphic design system that emulates the creative workflow of human designers. Building upon multiple specialized components, PSDesigner collects theme-related assets based on user instructions, and autonomously infers and executes tool calls to manipulate design files, such as integrating new assets or refining inferior elements. To endow the system with strong tool-use capabilities, we construct a design dataset, CreativePSD, which contains a large amount of high-quality PSD design files annotated with operation traces across a wide range of design scenarios and artistic styles, enabling models to learn expert design procedures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PSDesigner outperforms existing methods across diverse graphic design tasks, empowering non-specialists to conveniently create production-quality designs.

</details>


### [152] [MegaFlow: Zero-Shot Large Displacement Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25739)
*Dingxi Zhang,Fangjinhua Wang,Marc Pollefeys,Haofei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MegaFlow：利用预训练视觉Transformer特征进行全局匹配，实现零样本大位移光流估计的简单而强大的模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流估计方法依赖迭代局部搜索或领域特定微调，在大位移和零样本泛化场景中性能受限，需要一种能处理大位移且具有良好零样本泛化能力的新方法

Method: 将光流估计构建为全局匹配问题，利用预训练的全局Vision Transformer特征捕获大位移，然后通过轻量级迭代细化提高亚像素精度

Result: 在多个光流基准测试中实现最先进的零样本性能，在长距离点跟踪基准上也表现出高度竞争力的零样本性能，展示了强大的可迁移性

Conclusion: MegaFlow为通用化运动估计提供了一个统一的范式，通过利用预训练视觉先验而非复杂任务特定架构设计，实现了零样本大位移光流估计的突破

Abstract: Accurate estimation of large displacement optical flow remains a critical challenge. Existing methods typically rely on iterative local search or/and domain-specific fine-tuning, which severely limits their performance in large displacement and zero-shot generalization scenarios. To overcome this, we introduce MegaFlow, a simple yet powerful model for zero-shot large displacement optical flow. Rather than relying on highly complex, task-specific architectural designs, MegaFlow adapts powerful pre-trained vision priors to produce temporally consistent motion fields. In particular, we formulate flow estimation as a global matching problem by leveraging pre-trained global Vision Transformer features, which naturally capture large displacements. This is followed by a few lightweight iterative refinements to further improve the sub-pixel accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MegaFlow achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance across multiple optical flow benchmarks. Moreover, our model also delivers highly competitive zero-shot performance on long-range point tracking benchmarks, demonstrating its robust transferability and suggesting a unified paradigm for generalizable motion estimation. Our project page is at: https://kristen-z.github.io/projects/megaflow.

</details>


### [153] [RefAlign: Representation Alignment for Reference-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25743)
*Lei Wang,YuXin Song,Ge Wu,Haocheng Feng,Hang Zhou,Jingdong Wang,Yaxing Wang,jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefAlign是一个用于参考到视频生成任务的表示对齐框架，通过将扩散Transformer的参考分支特征与视觉基础模型的语义空间对齐，提升身份一致性和语义区分度，无需推理时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考到视频生成方法虽然引入了高层语义或跨模态特征来缓解像素级信息泄露，但仍难以解决异构编码器特征间的模态不匹配导致的复制粘贴伪影和多主体混淆问题。

Method: 提出RefAlign框架，核心是参考对齐损失函数，将扩散Transformer参考分支特征与视觉基础模型特征对齐：拉近相同主体的特征以提升身份一致性，推开不同主体的特征以增强语义区分度。仅在训练时应用，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval基准测试中，RefAlign在TotalScore上优于当前最先进方法，验证了显式参考对齐在参考到视频任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式对齐扩散Transformer参考特征与视觉基础模型语义空间能更好地平衡文本可控性和参考保真度，是解决参考到视频生成中模态不匹配问题的有效策略。

Abstract: Reference-to-video (R2V) generation is a controllable video synthesis paradigm that constrains the generation process using both text prompts and reference images, enabling applications such as personalized advertising and virtual try-on. In practice, existing R2V methods typically introduce additional high-level semantic or cross-modal features alongside the VAE latent representation of the reference image and jointly feed them into the diffusion Transformer (DiT). These auxiliary representations provide semantic guidance and act as implicit alignment signals, which can partially alleviate pixel-level information leakage in the VAE latent space. However, they may still struggle to address copy--paste artifacts and multi-subject confusion caused by modality mismatch across heterogeneous encoder features. In this paper, we propose RefAlign, a representation alignment framework that explicitly aligns DiT reference-branch features to the semantic space of a visual foundation model (VFM). The core of RefAlign is a reference alignment loss that pulls the reference features and VFM features of the same subject closer to improve identity consistency, while pushing apart the corresponding features of different subjects to enhance semantic discriminability. This simple yet effective strategy is applied only during training, incurring no inference-time overhead, and achieves a better balance between text controllability and reference fidelity. Extensive experiments on the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark demonstrate that RefAlign outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in TotalScore, validating the effectiveness of explicit reference alignment for R2V tasks.

</details>


### [154] [MuRF: Unlocking the Multi-Scale Potential of Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25744)
*Bocheng Zou,Mu Cai,Mark Stanley,Dingfu Lu,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MuRF方法，在推理时通过多分辨率融合提升视觉基础模型的性能，无需额外训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉基础模型在推理时通常使用单一固定尺度，忽略了不同分辨率提供互补归纳偏置的特性：低分辨率擅长全局语义识别，高分辨率适合细粒度优化

Method: MuRF（多分辨率融合）策略：在推理时对同一图像处理多个分辨率，通过冻结的视觉基础模型提取特征，然后将这些特征融合成统一表示

Result: MuRF具有普适性，不依赖特定架构，可作为无需训练的基础增强方法，已在DINOv2等多种视觉基础模型家族上验证有效，并能推广到SigLIP2等对比模型

Conclusion: 多分辨率融合是一种简单而有效的策略，能够利用不同分辨率的互补优势，提升视觉基础模型在各种计算机视觉任务中的表现

Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) have become the cornerstone of modern computer vision, offering robust representations across a wide array of tasks. While recent advances allow these models to handle varying input sizes during training, inference typically remains restricted to a single, fixed scale. This prevalent single-scale paradigm overlooks a fundamental property of visual perception: varying resolutions offer complementary inductive biases, where low-resolution views excel at global semantic recognition and high-resolution views are essential for fine-grained refinement. In this work, we propose Multi-Resolution Fusion (MuRF), a simple yet universally effective strategy to harness this synergy at inference time. Instead of relying on a single view, MuRF constructs a unified representation by processing an image at multiple resolutions through a frozen VFM and fusing the resulting features. The universality of MuRF is its most compelling attribute. It is not tied to a specific architecture, serving instead as a fundamental, training-free enhancement to visual representation. We empirically validate this by applying MuRF to a broad spectrum of critical computer vision tasks across multiple distinct VFM families - primarily DINOv2, but also demonstrating successful generalization to contrastive models like SigLIP2.

</details>


### [155] [Less Gaussians, Texture More: 4K Feed-Forward Textured Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25745)
*Yixing Lao,Xuyang Bai,Xiaoyang Wu,Nuoyuan Yan,Zixin Luo,Tian Fang,Jean-Daniel Nahmias,Yanghai Tsin,Shiwei Li,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LGTM提出了一种前馈式3D高斯溅射框架，通过预测紧凑的高斯基元配合每个基元的纹理，将几何复杂度与渲染分辨率解耦，实现了无需逐场景优化的4K新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D高斯溅射方法预测像素对齐的基元，导致基元数量随分辨率呈二次增长，限制了可扩展性，使4K等高分辨率合成变得不可行。

Method: LGTM框架预测紧凑的高斯基元，并为每个基元配备纹理，从而将几何复杂度与渲染分辨率解耦，避免了基元数量随分辨率二次增长的问题。

Result: LGTM实现了高保真的4K新视角合成，无需逐场景优化，这是前馈方法之前无法实现的能力，同时使用了显著更少的高斯基元。

Conclusion: LGTM突破了前馈式3D高斯溅射方法的分辨率缩放障碍，为高分辨率新视角合成提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods predict pixel-aligned primitives, leading to a quadratic growth in primitive count as resolution increases. This fundamentally limits their scalability, making high-resolution synthesis such as 4K intractable. We introduce LGTM (Less Gaussians, Texture More), a feed-forward framework that overcomes this resolution scaling barrier. By predicting compact Gaussian primitives coupled with per-primitive textures, LGTM decouples geometric complexity from rendering resolution. This approach enables high-fidelity 4K novel view synthesis without per-scene optimization, a capability previously out of reach for feed-forward methods, all while using significantly fewer Gaussian primitives. Project page: https://yxlao.github.io/lgtm/

</details>


### [156] [ShotStream: Streaming Multi-Shot Video Generation for Interactive Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25746)
*Yawen Luo,Xiaoyu Shi,Junhao Zhuang,Yutian Chen,Quande Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShotStream是一个因果多镜头视频生成架构，通过双向模型蒸馏和双缓存记忆机制，实现交互式实时叙事生成，达到单GPU 16FPS的速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前双向架构的多镜头视频生成存在交互性有限和延迟高的问题，无法满足实时交互式叙事的需求。需要一种能够支持动态指令输入、低延迟生成连贯多镜头视频的解决方案。

Method: 1) 将文本到视频模型微调为双向下一镜头生成器，然后通过分布匹配蒸馏到因果学生模型；2) 引入双缓存记忆机制：全局上下文缓存保持镜头间一致性，局部上下文缓存保持镜头内一致性；3) 使用RoPE不连续性指示器区分两种缓存；4) 采用两阶段蒸馏策略：从基于真实历史镜头的镜头内自强制开始，逐步扩展到使用自生成历史的镜头间自强制。

Result: ShotStream在单GPU上实现亚秒级延迟和16FPS的生成速度，生成连贯的多镜头视频，质量匹配或超过较慢的双向模型，为实时交互式叙事铺平道路。

Conclusion: ShotStream通过因果架构、双缓存记忆机制和两阶段蒸馏策略，成功解决了多镜头视频生成的交互性和延迟问题，实现了高效、连贯的实时叙事生成，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Multi-shot video generation is crucial for long narrative storytelling, yet current bidirectional architectures suffer from limited interactivity and high latency. We propose ShotStream, a novel causal multi-shot architecture that enables interactive storytelling and efficient on-the-fly frame generation. By reformulating the task as next-shot generation conditioned on historical context, ShotStream allows users to dynamically instruct ongoing narratives via streaming prompts. We achieve this by first fine-tuning a text-to-video model into a bidirectional next-shot generator, which is then distilled into a causal student via Distribution Matching Distillation. To overcome the challenges of inter-shot consistency and error accumulation inherent in autoregressive generation, we introduce two key innovations. First, a dual-cache memory mechanism preserves visual coherence: a global context cache retains conditional frames for inter-shot consistency, while a local context cache holds generated frames within the current shot for intra-shot consistency. And a RoPE discontinuity indicator is employed to explicitly distinguish the two caches to eliminate ambiguity. Second, to mitigate error accumulation, we propose a two-stage distillation strategy. This begins with intra-shot self-forcing conditioned on ground-truth historical shots and progressively extends to inter-shot self-forcing using self-generated histories, effectively bridging the train-test gap. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ShotStream generates coherent multi-shot videos with sub-second latency, achieving 16 FPS on a single GPU. It matches or exceeds the quality of slower bidirectional models, paving the way for real-time interactive storytelling. Training and inference code, as well as the models, are available on our

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Saranga: MilliWatt Ultrasound for Navigation in Visually Degraded Environments on Palm-Sized Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24699)
*Manoj Velmurugan,Phillip Brush,Colin Balfour,Richard J. Przybyla,Nitin J. Sanket*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Saranga：一种基于超声波的感知系统，使微型无人机能在视觉退化环境中导航，通过物理降噪和深度学习去噪方法解决低信噪比问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机在GPS拒止的视觉退化环境（如浓雾、黑暗、雪天）中导航困难，传统传感器（相机、激光雷达）失效，而雷达功耗过高。需要一种低功耗、能在恶劣视觉条件下工作的感知方案。

Method: 提出Saranga超声波感知系统：1）使用双声纳阵列定位障碍物；2）物理降噪方法阻挡螺旋桨引起的超声波噪声；3）深度学习去噪网络，利用超声波回声的长时域特征在强噪声中提取信号模式；4）使用合成数据生成管道和少量真实噪声数据进行训练。

Result: 成功使掌上无人机在浓雾、黑暗、雪天等视觉退化环境中，仅使用机载传感和计算，在包含薄型和透明障碍物的杂乱环境中实现导航。

Conclusion: Saranga系统通过超声波感知、物理降噪和深度学习去噪的组合方法，为微型无人机在视觉退化环境中的导航提供了有效的低功耗解决方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Tiny palm-sized aerial robots possess exceptional agility and cost-effectiveness in navigating confined and cluttered environments. However, their limited payload capacity directly constrains the sensing suite on-board the robot, thereby limiting critical navigational tasks in Global Positioning System (GPS)-denied wild scenes. Common methods for obstacle avoidance use cameras and LIght Detection And Ranging (LIDAR), which become ineffective in visually degraded conditions such as low visibility, dust, fog or darkness. Other sensors, such as RAdio Detection And Ranging (RADAR), have high power consumption, making them unsuitable for tiny aerial robots. Inspired by bats, we propose Saranga, a low-power ultrasound-based perception stack that localizes obstacles using a dual sonar array. We present two key solutions to combat the low Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio of $-4.9$ decibels: physical noise reduction and a deep learning based denoising method. Firstly, we present a practical way to block propeller induced ultrasound noise on the weak echoes. The second solution is to train a neural network to utilize the \textcolor{black}{long horizon of ultrasound echoes} for finding signal patterns under high amounts of uncorrelated noise where classical methods were insufficient. We generalize to the real world by using a synthetic data generation pipeline and limited real noise data for training. We enable a palm-sized aerial robot to navigate in visually degraded conditions of dense fog, darkness, and snow in a cluttered environment with thin and transparent obstacles using only on-board sensing and computation. We provide extensive real world results to demonstrate the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [158] [FODMP: Fast One-Step Diffusion of Movement Primitives Generation for Time-Dependent Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24806)
*Xirui Shi,Arya Ebrahimi,Yi Hu,Jun Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FODMP 提出了一种快速单步扩散运动基元框架，通过一致性蒸馏将扩散模型压缩到 ProDMPs 轨迹参数空间，在保持时间结构的同时实现高速推理，比现有方法快7-10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习中的扩散模型存在明显权衡：动作分块扩散策略（如ManiCM）推理快但只能预测短动作段，无法捕捉时间依赖的运动基元；而运动基元扩散（MPD）能生成时间结构运动但推理延迟过高，不适合实时控制。

Method: FODMP 将扩散模型蒸馏到概率动态运动基元（ProDMPs）的轨迹参数空间，使用单步解码器生成运动。通过一致性蒸馏消除推理瓶颈，保留运动基元的时间结构，实现高速推理。

Result: 在标准操作基准测试（MetaWorld, ManiSkill）中，FODMP 比 MPD 快10倍，比动作分块扩散策略快7倍，同时匹配或超过它们的成功率。还能生成快速加减速运动基元，实现实时拦截快速飞行球的任务。

Conclusion: FODMP 解决了扩散模型在机器人学习中的速度与时间结构权衡问题，通过单步一致性蒸馏实现了高速推理，同时保持运动基元的时间特性，适合闭环视觉控制应用。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used for robot learning, but current designs face a clear trade-off. Action-chunking diffusion policies like ManiCM are fast to run, yet they only predict short segments of motion. This makes them reactive, but unable to capture time-dependent motion primitives, such as following a spring-damper-like behavior with built-in dynamic profiles of acceleration and deceleration. Recently, Movement Primitive Diffusion (MPD) partially addresses this limitation by parameterizing full trajectories using Probabilistic Dynamic Movement Primitives (ProDMPs), thereby enabling the generation of temporally structured motions. Nevertheless, MPD integrates the motion decoder directly into a multi-step diffusion process, resulting in prohibitively high inference latency that limits its applicability in real-time control settings. We propose FODMP (Fast One-step Diffusion of Movement Primitives), a new framework that distills diffusion models into the ProDMPs trajectory parameter space and generates motion using a single-step decoder. FODMP retains the temporal structure of movement primitives while eliminating the inference bottleneck through single-step consistency distillation. This enables robots to execute time-dependent primitives at high inference speed, suitable for closed-loop vision-based control. On standard manipulation benchmarks (MetaWorld, ManiSkill), FODMP runs up to 10 times faster than MPD and 7 times faster than action-chunking diffusion policies, while matching or exceeding their success rates. Beyond speed, by generating fast acceleration-deceleration motion primitives, FODMP allows the robot to intercept and securely catch a fast-flying ball, whereas action-chunking diffusion policy and MPD respond too slowly for real-time interception.

</details>


### [159] [A Nonvolatile Switchable-polarity EPM Valve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24811)
*Bingchao Wang,Jonah Mack,Francesco Giorgio-Serchi,Adam A. Stokes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型双稳态电磁架构S-EPM，通过瞬态电激励可逆转变磁极性，无需持续供电，用于构建逻辑嵌入式流体网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前气动和流体网络的控制存在根本性限制：需要持续功率输入、密集的外部控制硬件和固定的路由拓扑。传统阀门阵列依赖连续驱动和机械固定路由，带来显著的热量和架构开销。

Method: 开发了可切换极性的电磁体(S-EPM)，通过重新配置复合磁体组件内部的磁通路径，建立两种稳定、相反的磁配置。将其集成到紧凑的夹管阀中，用于控制气动和液体介质。

Result: 实现了逻辑嵌入式流体网络，包括解码器、分层分配模块和非易失性六端口路由阵列。这些系统提供基于地址的路由和可编程组合控制，具备传统机械耦合旋转阀无法实现的端口隔离等功能。

Conclusion: 通过将功能嵌入持久磁状态而非持续功率或静态管道，这项工作为数字流体学和自主实验室平台建立了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Scalable control of pneumatic and fluidic networks remains fundamentally constrained by architectures that require continuous power input, dense external control hardware, and fixed routing topologies. Current valve arrays rely on such continuous actuation and mechanically fixed routing, imposing substantial thermal and architectural overhead. Here, we introduce the Switchable-polarity ElectroPermanent Magnet (S-EPM), a fundamentally new bistable magnetic architecture that deterministically reverses its external magnetic polarity through transient electrical excitation. By reconfiguring internal flux pathways within a composite magnet assembly, the S-EPM establishes two stable, opposing magnetic configurations without requiring sustained power. We integrate this architecture into a compact pinch-valve to robustly control pneumatic and liquid media. This state-encoded magnetic control enables logic-embedded fluidic networks, including decoders, hierarchical distribution modules, and a nonvolatile six-port routing array. These systems provide address-based routing and programmable compositional control, offering features like individual port isolation that are impossible with standard mechanically coupled rotary valves. By embedding functionality in persistent magnetic states rather than continuous power or static plumbing, this work establishes a scalable foundation for digital fluidics and autonomous laboratory platforms.

</details>


### [160] [Characterization of Constraints in Flexible Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24813)
*Samrat Bhattacharyya,Nabil Simaan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于在未知环境中安全自主操作柔性约束物体的在线路径规划算法，包括实时识别柔性约束和全局刚度的方法，通过仿真和实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中安全操作柔性约束物体（如手术中的器官牵引）需要实时识别约束特性，传统方法缺乏对柔性约束的实时探索和表征能力。

Method: 基于局部力和位置信息的约束探索方法，在物体轨迹的多个位姿处考虑感知约束刚度，使用刚度特征向量信息通过简单机械约束图集（如铰链、平面约束）来表征和识别全局刚度行为。

Result: 算法能够在实时识别常见简单机械约束（如柔性铰链）并识别相关螺旋参数，验证了同时进行全局约束/刚度探索和安全操作柔性约束物体的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为实现安全协作操作（如手术中的器官牵引和操作）提供了可行途径，展示了在未知环境中同时探索、表征和操作弹性系统的能力。

Abstract: This paper presents an online path planning algorithm for safe autonomous manipulation of a flexibly constrained object in an unknown environment. Methods for real time identification and characterization of perceived flexible constraints and global stiffness are presented. Used in tandem, these methods allow a robot to simultaneously explore, characterize, and manipulate an elastic system safely. Navigation without a-priori knowledge of the system is achieved using constraint exploration based on local force and position information. The perceived constraint stiffness is considered at multiple poses along an object's (system) trajectory. Using stiffness eigenvector information, global stiffness behavior is characterized and identified using an atlas of simple mechanical constraints, such as hinges and planar constraints. Validation of these algorithms is carried out by simulation and experimentally. The ability to recognize several common simple mechanical constraints (such as a flexible hinge) in real time, and to subsequently identify relevant screw parameters is demonstrated. These results suggest the feasibility of simultaneous global constrain/stiffness exploration and safe manipulation of flexibly constrained objects. We believe that this approach will eventually enable safe cooperative manipulation in applications such as organ retraction and manipulation during surgery

</details>


### [161] [Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation, Sequencing, and Optimal Trajectory Generation in Obstacle-Rich 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24908)
*Yunes Alqudsi,Murat Makaraci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMD-TAPP是一个端到端框架，用于协调无人机团队在复杂3D环境中执行任务，联合解决多目标分配、路径规划和轨迹生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂三维环境中协调无人机团队需要将离散的任务规划（决定哪个无人机服务哪个目标以及顺序）与连续时间轨迹合成（确保避碰和动态可行性）进行原则性集成。

Method: 1) 将工作空间离散化为3D导航图，通过图搜索计算机器人到目标和目标间避障成本；2) 使用注入粒子群优化(IPSO)方案，结合多重线性分配，探索分配/排序组合以最小化任务完成时间；3) 将航点路径转换为时间参数化的最小snap轨迹，通过生成-优化循环验证障碍物间隙和机器人间距，违反安全边界时触发重新规划。

Result: MATLAB仿真显示IMD-TAPP在复杂3D场景中能持续生成动态可行、无碰撞的轨迹，同时实现有竞争力的完成时间。在双无人机服务多目标的案例中，该方法达到136秒的最小任务时间，并全程保持所需安全约束。

Conclusion: IMD-TAPP提供了一个有效的端到端框架，能够联合解决无人机团队在复杂环境中的任务分配和路径规划问题，生成安全且高效的轨迹。

Abstract: Coordinating teams of aerial robots in cluttered three-dimensional (3D) environments requires a principled integration of discrete mission planning-deciding which robot serves which goals and in what order -- with continuous-time trajectory synthesis that enforces collision avoidance and dynamic feasibility. This paper introduces IMD-TAPP (Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation and Path Planning), an end-to-end framework that jointly addresses multi-goal allocation, tour sequencing, and safe trajectory generation for quadrotor teams operating in obstacle-rich spaces. IMD--TAPP first discretizes the workspace into a 3D navigation graph and computes obstacle-aware robot-to-goal and goal-to-goal travel costs via graph-search-based pathfinding. These costs are then embedded within an Injected Particle Swarm Optimization (IPSO) scheme, guided by multiple linear assignment, to efficiently explore coupled assignment/ordering alternatives and to minimize mission makespan. Finally, the resulting waypoint tours are transformed into time-parameterized minimum-snap trajectories through a generation-and-optimization routine equipped with iterative validation of obstacle clearance and inter-robot separation, triggering re-planning when safety margins are violated. Extensive MATLAB simulations across cluttered 3D scenarios demonstrate that IMD--TAPP consistently produces dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories while achieving competitive completion times. In a representative case study with two drones serving multiple goals, the proposed approach attains a minimum mission time of 136~s while maintaining the required safety constraints throughout execution.

</details>


### [162] [CROSS: A Mixture-of-Experts Reinforcement Learning Framework for Generalizable Large-Scale Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24930)
*Xibei Chen,Yifeng Zhang,Yuxiang Xiao,Mingfeng Fan,Maonan Wang,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CROSS框架，一种基于混合专家(MoE)的去中心化强化学习方法，用于可泛化的自适应交通信号控制，通过预测对比聚类和场景自适应MoE模块提升对多样化交通模式的表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的自适应交通信号控制方法通常对所有场景使用单一共享策略，其有限的表示能力难以捕捉多样化的交通动态并泛化到未见环境，需要解决大规模可泛化ATSC的挑战。

Method: 提出CROSS框架：1) 预测对比聚类模块，通过预测短期状态转移识别潜在交通模式，结合聚类和对比学习增强模式级表示；2) 场景自适应MoE模块，用多个专家增强共享策略，实现自适应专业化和灵活的特定场景策略。

Result: 在SUMO模拟器上对合成和真实世界交通数据集进行广泛实验，相比最先进的基线方法，CROSS通过改进对多样化交通场景的表示，实现了优越的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: CROSS框架通过结合预测对比聚类和场景自适应混合专家模块，有效解决了大规模自适应交通信号控制的泛化挑战，能够更好地表示和处理多样化的交通动态。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotics, automation, and artificial intelligence have enabled urban traffic systems to operate with increasing autonomy towards future smart cities, powered in part by the development of adaptive traffic signal control (ATSC), which dynamically optimizes signal phases to mitigate congestion and optimize traffic. However, achieving effective and generalizable large-scale ATSC remains a significant challenge due to the diverse intersection topologies and highly dynamic, complex traffic demand patterns across the network. Existing RL-based methods typically use a single shared policy for all scenarios, whose limited representational capacity makes it difficult to capture diverse traffic dynamics and generalize to unseen environments. To address these challenges, we propose CROSS, a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based decentralized RL framework for generalizable ATSC. We first introduce a Predictive Contrastive Clustering (PCC) module that forecasts short-term state transitions to identify latent traffic patterns, followed by clustering and contrastive learning to enhance pattern-level representation. We further design a Scenario-Adaptive MoE module that augments a shared policy with multiple experts, thus enabling adaptive specialization and more flexible scenario-specific strategies. We conduct extensive experiments in the SUMO simulator on both synthetic and real-world traffic datasets. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, CROSS achieves superior performance and generalization through improved representation of diverse traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [163] [COIN: Collaborative Interaction-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Self-Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24931)
*Yifeng Zhang,Jieming Chen,Tingguang Zhou,Tanishq Duhan,Jianghong Dong,Yuhong Cao,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COIN是一个用于多智能体自动驾驶的协作交互感知MARL框架，通过CIG-TD3算法和双级交互感知中心化评论家架构，在密集交通场景中实现安全高效的协同驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体自动驾驶系统能协调自动驾驶车辆减少拥堵、提高安全和效率，但如何在密集场景中实现高效安全的协作仍面临挑战，特别是在复杂智能体交互的动态环境中。

Method: 提出COIN框架，包含：1）CIG-TD3算法，采用"集中训练、分散执行"方式，联合优化个体导航目标和全局协作目标；2）双级交互感知中心化评论家架构，捕捉局部成对交互和全局系统级依赖，提高全局价值估计和信用分配准确性。

Result: 在密集城市交通环境中的大量仿真实验表明，COIN在各种系统规模下，在安全和效率方面均优于其他先进基线方法。真实世界机器人演示进一步验证了其在复杂动态MASD场景中的优越性。

Conclusion: COIN框架通过创新的协作交互感知MARL方法，有效解决了密集多智能体自动驾驶场景中的协同挑战，在安全性和效率方面表现出色，为未来智能交通系统提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Self-Driving (MASD) systems provide an effective solution for coordinating autonomous vehicles to reduce congestion and enhance both safety and operational efficiency in future intelligent transportation systems. Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has emerged as a promising approach for developing advanced end-to-end MASD systems. However, achieving efficient and safe collaboration in dynamic MASD systems remains a significant challenge in dense scenarios with complex agent interactions. To address this challenge, we propose a novel collaborative(CO-) interaction-aware(-IN) MARL framework, named COIN. Specifically, we develop a new counterfactual individual-global twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient (CIG-TD3) algorithm, crafted in a "centralized training, decentralized execution" (CTDE) manner, which aims to jointly optimize the individual objectives (navigation) and the global objectives (collaboration) of agents. We further introduce a dual-level interaction-aware centralized critic architecture that captures both local pairwise interactions and global system-level dependencies, enabling more accurate global value estimation and improved credit assignment for collaborative policy learning. We conduct extensive simulation experiments in dense urban traffic environments, which demonstrate that COIN consistently outperforms other advanced baseline methods in both safety and efficiency across various system sizes. These results highlight its superiority in complex and dynamic MASD scenarios, as further validated through real-world robot demonstrations. Supplementary videos are available at https://marmotlab.github.io/COIN/

</details>


### [164] [SABER: A Stealthy Agentic Black-Box Attack Framework for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24935)
*Xiyang Wu,Guangyao Shi,Qingzi Wang,Zongxia Li,Amrit Singh Bedi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SABER是一个基于智能体的黑盒攻击方法，通过生成微小但有效的指令编辑来评估VLA模型的鲁棒性，在有限编辑预算下显著降低机器人任务成功率并增加执行长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然能让机器人理解自然语言指令，但指令通道也引入了安全漏洞：微小的文本扰动就能改变机器人行为。需要系统性的鲁棒性评估方法来生成跨VLA模型的最小但有效的指令编辑攻击。

Method: SABER使用GRPO训练的ReAct攻击者，在有限编辑预算下通过字符级、词元级和提示级工具生成小而合理的对抗性指令编辑，诱导目标行为退化（任务失败、执行过长、约束违反增加）。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试的六个最先进VLA模型上，SABER将任务成功率降低20.6%，动作序列长度增加55%，约束违反增加33%，同时比GPT基线减少21.1%的工具调用和54.7%的字符编辑。

Conclusion: 微小合理的指令编辑足以显著降低机器人执行性能，基于智能体的黑盒流水线为红队测试机器人基础模型提供了实用、可扩展和自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural-language instructions grounded in visual observations, but the instruction channel also introduces a critical vulnerability: small textual perturbations can alter downstream robot behavior. Systematic robustness evaluation therefore requires a black-box attacker that can generate minimal yet effective instruction edits across diverse VLA models. To this end, we present SABER, an agent-centric approach for automatically generating instruction-based adversarial attacks on VLA models under bounded edit budgets. SABER uses a GRPO-trained ReAct attacker to generate small, plausible adversarial instruction edits using character-, token-, and prompt-level tools under a bounded edit budget that induces targeted behavioral degradation, including task failure, unnecessarily long execution, and increased constraint violations. On the LIBERO benchmark across six state-of-the-art VLA models, SABER reduces task success by 20.6%, increases action-sequence length by 55%, and raises constraint violations by 33%, while requiring 21.1% fewer tool calls and 54.7% fewer character edits than strong GPT-based baselines. These results show that small, plausible instruction edits are sufficient to substantially degrade robot execution, and that an agentic black-box pipeline offers a practical, scalable, and adaptive approach for red-teaming robotic foundation models.

</details>


### [165] [Wireless bioelectronics for untethered biohybrid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24959)
*Hiroyuki Tetsuka,Minoru Hirano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了生物混合机器人控制策略的最新进展，包括无线电刺激、无线光刺激和神经肌肉整合，并展望了神经类器官-生物电子学共集成的发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物混合机器人将活体组织与工程结构结合以实现仿生驱动，但传统的有线连接和浸入式硬件限制了其应用。需要无线控制技术来解决信号传输问题，实现更灵活、自主的生物混合机器人系统。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，总结三种主要控制策略：1）无线生物电子学通过射频磁场远程传输控制信号刺激肌肉组织；2）无线光电子学通过嵌入式光源远程控制光遗传修饰的肌肉机器人；3）神经肌肉整合利用生物信号转导实现多执行器的选择性控制。

Result: 文章系统梳理了生物混合机器人控制领域的最新进展，展示了无线电刺激、无线光刺激和神经肌肉整合等技术如何解决传统控制方法的局限性，为生物混合机器人的实际应用提供了技术基础。

Conclusion: 未来发展方向是神经类器官与生物电子学的共集成，这将推动生物混合机器人向自主、闭环控制系统发展，实现更复杂的仿生行为和智能功能。

Abstract: Biohybrid robots integrate living tissues with engineered artificial structures to achieve organism-inspired actuation and behavior. A persistent challenge is delivering stimulation and control signals without relying on tethered wiring or bulky hardware immersed in cell-culture media. Wireless bioelectronics addresses this limitation by enabling the remote transfer of control signals, typically via radio-frequency magnetic fields, to locally stimulate muscle tissues at tissue-electrode interfaces. In parallel, wireless optoelectronics enables remote control of optogenetically modified, muscle-based robots by embedding light emitters that initiate muscle actuation through light-gated ion channels. Further advances incorporate neuromuscular junctions, leveraging biological signal transduction to enable selective control of multiple actuators through wireless frequency- and time-division multiplexing. This perspective article summarizes recent advances in control strategies for biohybrid robots, namely, wireless electrical stimulation, wireless optical stimulation, and neuromuscular integration. Then this describes cross-cutting design principles and highlights a future direction, namely, co-integration of neural organoid-bioelectronics toward autonomous, closed-loop biohybrid robots.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Rollout from Sampling:An R1-Style Tokenized Traffic Simulation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24989)
*Ziyan Wang,Peng Chen,Ding Li,Chiwei Li,Qichao Zhang,Zhongpu Xia,Guizhen Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R1Sim：基于运动令牌熵模式的强化学习交通仿真方法，通过熵引导自适应采样和GRPO优化，实现真实、安全、多样的多智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NTP范式的交通仿真方法限制了主动探索，特别是在次优区域。熵模式为基于运动令牌不确定性的探索提供了新视角，需要探索强化学习在交通仿真中的应用。

Method: 提出R1Sim令牌化交通仿真策略：1）熵引导自适应采样机制，关注高不确定性但高潜力的运动令牌；2）使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）优化运动行为；3）安全感知的奖励设计。

Result: 在Waymo Sim Agent基准测试中，R1Sim实现了与最先进方法相竞争的性能，能够生成真实、安全、多样的多智能体行为。

Conclusion: R1Sim首次探索了基于运动令牌熵模式的强化学习交通仿真，通过平衡探索-利用权衡，显著提升了交通仿真的多样性和真实性。

Abstract: Learning diverse and high-fidelity traffic simulations from human driving demonstrations is crucial for autonomous driving evaluation. The recent next-token prediction (NTP) paradigm, widely adopted in large language models (LLMs), has been applied to traffic simulation and achieves iterative improvements via supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, such methods limit active exploration of potentially valuable motion tokens, particularly in suboptimal regions. Entropy patterns provide a promising perspective for enabling exploration driven by motion token uncertainty. Motivated by this insight, we propose a novel tokenized traffic simulation policy, R1Sim, which represents an initial attempt to explore reinforcement learning based on motion token entropy patterns, and systematically analyzes the impact of different motion tokens on simulation outcomes. Specifically, we introduce an entropy-guided adaptive sampling mechanism that focuses on previously overlooked motion tokens with high uncertainty yet high potential. We further optimize motion behaviors using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), guided by a safety-aware reward design. Overall, these components enable a balanced exploration-exploitation trade-off through diverse high-uncertainty sampling and group-wise comparative estimation, resulting in realistic, safe, and diverse multi-agent behaviors. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Sim Agent benchmark demonstrate that R1Sim achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [167] [$π$, But Make It Fly: Physics-Guided Transfer of VLA Models to Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25038)
*Johnathan Tucker,Denis Liu,Aiden Swann,Allen Ren,Javier Yu,Jiankai Sun,Brandon Kim,Lachlain McGranahan,Quan Vuong,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirVLA系统将地面机械臂预训练的视觉语言动作模型迁移到空中无人机平台，通过载荷感知引导和合成数据训练解决动力学差异问题，显著提升了空中抓取放置任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLA模型在固定基座机械臂上表现出色，但将其迁移到空中平台面临挑战，因为地面机械臂的准静态动力学与飞行的欠驱动、高动态特性存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 1) 引入载荷感知引导机制，将载荷约束直接注入策略的流匹配采样过程；2) 使用高斯泼溅管道合成导航训练数据以克服数据稀缺问题。

Result: 合成数据使导航任务成功率从81%提升至100%；载荷感知引导将真实世界抓取放置任务成功率从23%提升至50%；长时域组合任务总体成功率达62%。

Conclusion: 预训练的机械臂VLA模型通过适当的数据增强和物理信息引导，可以成功迁移到空中操作和导航任务，并能组合执行这些任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models such as $π_0$ have demonstrated remarkable generalization across diverse fixed-base manipulators. However, transferring these foundation models to aerial platforms remains an open challenge due to the fundamental mismatch between the quasi-static dynamics of fixed-base arms and the underactuated, highly dynamic nature of flight. In this work, we introduce AirVLA, a system that investigates the transferability of manipulation-pretrained VLAs to aerial pick-and-place tasks. We find that while visual representations transfer effectively, the specific control dynamics required for flight do not. To bridge this "dynamics gap" without retraining the foundation model, we introduce a Payload-Aware Guidance mechanism that injects payload constraints directly into the policy's flow-matching sampling process. To overcome data scarcity, we further utilize a Gaussian Splatting pipeline to synthesize navigation training data. We evaluate our method through a cumulative 460 real-world experiments which demonstrate that this synthetic data is a key enabler of performance, unlocking 100% success in navigation tasks where directly fine-tuning on teleoperation data alone attains 81% success. Our inference-time intervention, Payload-Aware Guidance, increases real-world pick-and-place task success from 23% to 50%. Finally, we evaluate the model on a long-horizon compositional task, achieving a 62% overall success rate. These results suggest that pre-trained manipulation VLAs, with appropriate data augmentation and physics-informed guidance, can transfer to aerial manipulation and navigation, as well as the composition of these tasks.

</details>


### [168] [ThermoAct:Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Perception and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25044)
*Young-Chae Son,Dae-Kwan Ko,Yoon-Ji Choi,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合热信息的VLA框架，通过VLM作为高层规划器解析自然语言指令并分解任务，结合热感知提升机器人安全性和任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作环境需要超越视觉的多传感器数据集成以提升安全性和智能性，但热数据集成在先前研究中相对被忽视，而热信息对机器人安全和操作效率至关重要

Method: 提出新颖的Vision-Language-Action（VLA）框架，集成热信息用于机器人任务执行；利用Vision-Language Model（VLM）作为高层规划器解析复杂自然语言指令并分解为简单子任务；不同于仅依赖视觉数据的传统方法，本方法整合热信息使机器人能感知物理特性并主动确保环境安全

Result: 真实世界任务场景的实验结果验证了所提框架的可行性，表明相比现有视觉系统，本方法有潜力提升任务成功率和安全性

Conclusion: 通过集成热信息到VLA框架中，能够增强机器人的环境感知能力和安全性，为人机协作提供更智能、安全的解决方案

Abstract: In recent human-robot collaboration environments, there is a growing focus on integrating diverse sensor data beyond visual information to enable safer and more intelligent task execution. Although thermal data can be crucial for enhancing robot safety and operational efficiency, its integration has been relatively overlooked in prior research. This paper proposes a novel Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that incorporates thermal information for robot task execution. The proposed system leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as a high-level planner to interpret complex natural language commands and decompose them into simpler sub-tasks. This approach facilitates efficient data collection and robust reasoning for complex operations. Unlike conventional methods that rely solely on visual data, our approach integrates thermal information, enabling the robot to perceive physical properties and proactively ensure environmental safety. Experimental results from real-world task scenarios validate the feasibility of our proposed framework, suggesting its potential to enhance task success rates and safety compared to existing vision-based systems.

</details>


### [169] [CTS-PLL: A Robust and Anytime Framework for Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25121)
*Junkai Jiang,Yitao Xu,Ruochen Li,Shaobing Xu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CTS-PLL：一个用于协作任务排序和多智能体路径规划的分层框架，通过锁智能体检测与释放机制以及基于大邻域搜索的随时优化，提高了密集环境下的鲁棒性和解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTS-MAPF问题要求智能体在避免碰撞的同时完成一系列任务，由于其组合复杂性而面临重大挑战。现有方法在密集环境中鲁棒性不足，解质量有待提高。

Method: 提出了CTS-PLL分层框架，扩展了基于配置的CTS-MAPF规划范式：1）锁智能体检测与释放机制，利用完整规划方法进行局部重规划；2）基于大邻域搜索（LNS）的随时优化程序。

Result: 在稀疏和密集基准测试中，CTS-PLL相比现有方法实现了更高的成功率和解质量，同时保持了有竞争力的运行时间效率。真实机器人实验进一步验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: CTS-PLL通过创新的锁智能体机制和随时优化策略，有效解决了CTS-MAPF问题在密集环境中的挑战，为实际机器人应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding (CTS-MAPF) problem requires agents to accomplish sequences of tasks while avoiding collisions, posing significant challenges due to its combinatorial complexity. This work introduces CTS-PLL, a hierarchical framework that extends the configuration-based CTS-MAPF planning paradigm with two key enhancements: a lock agents detection and release mechanism leveraging a complete planning method for local re-planning, and an anytime refinement procedure based on Large Neighborhood Search (LNS). These additions ensure robustness in dense environments and enable continuous improvement of solution quality. Extensive evaluations across sparse and dense benchmarks demonstrate that CTS-PLL achieves higher success rates and solution quality compared with existing methods, while maintaining competitive runtime efficiency. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of the approach in practice.

</details>


### [170] [Dissimilarity-Based Persistent Coverage Control of Multi-Robot Systems for Improving Solar Irradiance Prediction Accuracy in Solar Thermal Power Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25139)
*Haruki Kawase,Taiga Sugawara,A. Daniel Carnerero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于克里金模型的不相似度地图和持续覆盖控制算法，用于引导移动传感器优化太阳能辐照度预测精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有克里金预测方法缺乏动态部署移动传感器的采样策略，无法在实时优化预测精度时最小化传感器数量

Method: 从克里金模型推导出不相似度地图，提出持续覆盖控制算法，引导移动代理到需要额外观测的区域以改进预测性能

Result: 通过移动机器人实验验证，在各种模拟辐照度场下，该方法比基准方法获得更准确的预测

Conclusion: 提出的不相似度地图和持续覆盖控制算法能有效优化移动传感器部署，提高太阳能辐照度预测精度

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of future solar irradiance is essential for the effective control of solar thermal power plants. Although various kriging-based methods have been proposed to address the prediction problem, these methods typically do not provide an appropriate sampling strategy to dynamically position mobile sensors for optimizing prediction accuracy in real time, which is critical for achieving accurate forecasts with a minimal number of sensors. This paper introduces a dissimilarity map derived from a kriging model and proposes a persistent coverage control algorithm that effectively guides agents toward regions where additional observations are required to improve prediction performance. By means of experiments using mobile robots, the proposed approach was shown to obtain more accurate predictions than the considered baselines under various emulated irradiance fields.

</details>


### [171] [A Minimum-Energy Control Approach for Redundant Mobile Manipulators in Physical Human-Robot Interaction Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25259)
*Davide Tebaldi,Niccolò Paradisi,Fabio Pini,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于移动机械臂的控制方法，旨在最小化人机物理交互中系统的总动能，通过实验验证在插孔任务中能降低系统动能并提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂系统在人机物理交互领域快速发展，但相比固定基座机械臂，移动机械臂引入了额外自由度，使得控制方法设计更具挑战性且更需性能优化。需要开发适合的控制方法来处理这些挑战。

Method: 提出一种移动机械臂控制方法，该机械臂由移动基座和顶部安装的机械臂组成。控制目标是在人机物理交互应用中最小化整个移动机械臂系统的总动能。

Result: 通过插孔任务进行实验测试，结果表明所提出的方法降低了整个机械系统的总动能，并且相比基准方法提升了系统性能。

Conclusion: 该控制方法能有效优化移动机械臂在人机物理交互中的性能，通过最小化系统动能改善了系统表现，为移动机械臂控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Research on mobile manipulation systems that physically interact with humans has expanded rapidly in recent years, opening the way to tasks which could not be performed using fixed-base manipulators. Within this context, developing suitable control methodologies is essential since mobile manipulators introduce additional degrees of freedom, making the design of control approaches more challenging and more prone to performance optimization. This paper proposes a control approach for a mobile manipulator, composed of a mobile base equipped with a robotic arm mounted on the top, with the objective of minimizing the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body mobile manipulator in physical human-robot interaction applications. The approach is experimentally tested with reference to a peg-in-hole task, and the results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body robotic system and improves the system performance compared with the benchmark method.

</details>


### [172] [Connectivity-Aware Representations for Constrained Motion Planning via Multi-Scale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25298)
*Suhyun Jeon,Yumin Lim,Woo-Jeong Baek,Hyeonseo Kim,Suhan Park,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于连通性感知表示学习的运动规划方法，通过多尺度流形学习和对比学习框架，选择位于连通区域的起始和目标配置，避免本质互不连通组件问题，提高规划成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束运动规划面临本质互不连通组件（EMD）问题，约束将可行空间限制到低维子流形，冗余性引入额外复杂性，导致规划效率低下甚至不可行。需要一种方法能够预先识别配置之间的连通性。

Method: 通过多尺度流形学习将关节配置嵌入到潜在空间，从局部到全局的邻域范围学习表示，通过聚类生成伪标签，监督对比学习框架，获得连通性感知的度量，用于选择位于连通区域的起始和目标配置。

Result: 在各种操作任务实验中，该方法相比基线实现了1.9倍的成功率提升，规划时间减少了0.43倍，显著提高了运动规划的成功率和效率。

Conclusion: 提出的连通性感知表示学习框架能够有效识别配置之间的连通性，避免EMD问题，为约束运动规划提供了一种高效的前处理方案，显著提升规划性能。

Abstract: The objective of constrained motion planning is to connect start and goal configurations while satisfying task-specific constraints. Motion planning becomes inefficient or infeasible when the configurations lie in disconnected regions, known as essentially mutually disconnected (EMD) components. Constraints further restrict feasible space to a lower-dimensional submanifold, while redundancy introduces additional complexity because a single end-effector pose admits infinitely many inverse kinematic solutions that may form discrete self-motion manifolds. This paper addresses these challenges by learning a connectivity-aware representation for selecting start and goal configurations prior to planning. Joint configurations are embedded into a latent space through multi-scale manifold learning across neighborhood ranges from local to global, and clustering generates pseudo-labels that supervise a contrastive learning framework. The proposed framework provides a connectivity-aware measure that biases the selection of start and goal configurations in connected regions, avoiding EMDs and yielding higher success rates with reduced planning time. Experiments on various manipulation tasks showed that our method achieves 1.9 times higher success rates and reduces the planning time by a factor of 0.43 compared to baselines.

</details>


### [173] [SafeGuard ASF: SR Agentic Humanoid Robot System for Autonomous Industrial Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25353)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Tuan Tran,Ben Wei Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeGuard ASF是一个部署人形机器人进行工业环境自主危险检测的框架，整合多模态感知、ReAct智能体推理和学习运动策略，针对火灾烟雾、管道异常温度和入侵者检测三种危险场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人"黑灯工厂"的兴起，需要能够检测和响应多种危险类型的自主安全系统。传统安全系统在无人环境中存在局限性，需要更智能的自主解决方案。

Method: 采用Unitree G1人形机器人平台，整合RGB-D多模态感知、基于ReAct的智能体推理框架和学习运动策略。感知管道实现94.2% mAP的火灾烟雾检测，使用Unitree RL Lab和PPO训练多种运动策略，包括舞蹈运动跟踪和速度控制。

Result: 感知管道在127ms延迟下达到94.2% mAP的火灾烟雾检测性能。运动策略在80,000次训练迭代内稳定收敛。在仿真和真实环境中验证了自主巡逻、人类检测和避障能力。ToolOrchestra行动框架支持结构化决策。

Conclusion: SafeGuard ASF框架成功展示了人形机器人在工业环境中进行自主危险检测的可行性，为无人工厂的安全监控提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The rise of unmanned ``dark factories'' operating without human presence demands autonomous safety systems capable of detecting and responding to multiple hazard types. We present SafeGuard ASF (Agentic Security Fleet), a comprehensive framework deploying humanoid robots for autonomous hazard detection in industrial environments. Our system integrates multi-modal perception (RGB-D imaging), a ReAct-based agentic reasoning framework, and learned locomotion policies on the Unitree G1 humanoid platform. We address three critical hazard scenarios: fire and smoke detection, abnormal temperature monitoring in pipelines, and intruder detection in restricted zones. Our perception pipeline achieves 94.2% mAP for fire or smoke detection with 127ms latency. We train multiple locomotion policies, including dance motion tracking and velocity control, using Unitree RL Lab with PPO, demonstrating stable convergence within 80,000 training iterations. We validate our system in both simulation and real-world environments, demonstrating autonomous patrol, human detection with visual perception, and obstacle avoidance capabilities. The proposed ToolOrchestra action framework enables structured decision-making through perception, reasoning, and actuation tools.

</details>


### [174] [Bayesian Learning-Enhanced Navigation with Deep Smoothing for Inertial-Aided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25364)
*Nadav Cohen,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BLENDS是一个结合贝叶斯学习与深度平滑的数据驱动后处理框架，通过transformer网络修改滤波器协方差矩阵并添加误差状态修正，显著提升INS/GNSS导航精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定区间平滑算法在松散耦合INS/GNSS系统中无法解决GNSS原始测量固有的系统性位置偏差问题，导致精度存在持续差距。

Method: 提出BLENDS框架，将经典双滤波器平滑器与基于transformer的神经网络结合，学习修改滤波器协方差矩阵并在贝叶斯框架内直接对平滑误差状态添加修正，使用贝叶斯一致损失函数联合监督平滑均值和协方差。

Result: 在移动机器人和四旋翼无人机两个真实世界数据集上评估，在所有未见测试轨迹上，BLENDS相比基线前向EKF实现了高达63%的水平位置精度提升。

Conclusion: BLENDS成功将数据驱动方法与贝叶斯平滑相结合，有效解决了传统模型平滑器无法克服的GNSS系统性偏差问题，显著提升了后处理导航精度。

Abstract: Accurate post-processing navigation is essential for applications such as survey and mapping, where the full measurement history can be exploited to refine past state estimates. Fixed-interval smoothing algorithms represent the theoretically optimal solution under Gaussian assumptions. However, loosely coupled INS/GNSS systems fundamentally inherit the systematic position bias of raw GNSS measurements, leaving a persistent accuracy gap that model-based smoothers cannot resolve. To address this limitation, we propose BLENDS, which integrates Bayesian learning with deep smoothing to enhance navigation performance. BLENDS is a a data-driven post-processing framework that augments the classical two-filter smoother with a transformer-based neural network. It learns to modify the filter covariance matrices and apply an additive correction to the smoothed error-state directly within the Bayesian framework. A novel Bayesian-consistent loss jointly supervises the smoothed mean and covariance, enforcing minimum-variance estimates while maintaining statistical consistency. BLENDS is evaluated on two real-world datasets spanning a mobile robot and a quadrotor. Across all unseen test trajectories, BLENDS achieves horizontal position improvements of up to 63% over the baseline forward EKF.

</details>


### [175] [Integrating Deep RL and Bayesian Inference for ObjectNav in Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25366)
*João Castelo-Branco,José Santos-Victor,Alexandre Bernardino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合贝叶斯推理与深度强化学习的混合物体搜索框架，通过维护目标位置的空间信念地图，训练强化学习策略进行导航决策，在室内环境中提高了搜索成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在室内环境进行自主物体搜索面临部分可观测性、感知不确定性和探索-导航效率权衡的挑战。传统概率方法依赖人工设计的动作选择启发式，而深度强化学习方法收敛慢且可解释性有限。

Method: 提出混合物体搜索框架：1) 通过贝叶斯推理维护目标位置的空间信念地图，在线更新校准后的物体检测结果；2) 训练强化学习策略直接从概率表示中选择导航动作。

Result: 在Habitat 3.0模拟的两个室内环境中进行评估，相比基线策略，该方法提高了搜索成功率，同时减少了搜索工作量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将贝叶斯信念估计与学习型动作选择相结合，能够在部分可观测条件下实现更高效可靠的物体搜索行为。

Abstract: Autonomous object search is challenging for mobile robots operating in indoor environments due to partial observability, perceptual uncertainty, and the need to trade off exploration and navigation efficiency. Classical probabilistic approaches explicitly represent uncertainty but typically rely on handcrafted action-selection heuristics, while deep reinforcement learning enables adaptive policies but often suffers from slow convergence and limited interpretability. This paper proposes a hybrid object-search framework that integrates Bayesian inference with deep reinforcement learning. The method maintains a spatial belief map over target locations, updated online through Bayesian inference from calibrated object detections, and trains a reinforcement learning policy to select navigation actions directly from this probabilistic representation. The approach is evaluated in realistic indoor simulation using Habitat 3.0 and compared against developed baseline strategies. Across two indoor environments, the proposed method improves success rate while reducing search effort. Overall, the results support the value of combining Bayesian belief estimation with learned action selection to achieve more efficient and reliable objectsearch behavior under partial observability.

</details>


### [176] [IntentReact: Guiding Reactive Object-Centric Navigation via Topological Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25382)
*Yanmei Jiao,Anpeng Lu,Wenhan Hu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang,Huajin Tang,Wen-an Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IntentReact：一种意图条件化的物体中心导航框架，通过将全局拓扑规划编码为低维方向信号（意图），来指导局部感知驱动的控制，解决导航中全局拓扑引导与局部控制之间的差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物体级拓扑地图的导航方法存在全局拓扑引导与局部感知驱动控制之间的差距。局部决策仅基于当前自我中心观测，缺乏视野外信息，导致机器人可能持续朝错误方向移动，无法减少全局拓扑距离。

Method: 提出IntentReact框架，将全局拓扑引导编码为低维方向信号（意图），该意图条件化地指导学习到的路径点预测策略，使导航偏向拓扑一致的方向。这种设计使机器人能够在局部观测误导时及时重新定向。

Result: 通过大量实验评估，相比先前的物体中心导航方法，该框架在导航成功率和执行质量方面都有显著提升。

Conclusion: IntentReact通过紧凑的意图接口有效桥接了全局拓扑规划与反应式物体中心控制，既保持了物体中心控制的反应性和鲁棒性，又实现了全局拓扑一致的导航。

Abstract: Object-goal visual navigation requires robots to reason over semantic structure and act effectively under partial observability. Recent approaches based on object-level topological maps enable long-horizon navigation without dense geometric reconstruction, but their execution remains limited by the gap between global topological guidance and local perception-driven control. In particular, local decisions are made solely from the current egocentric observation, without access to information beyond the robot's field of view. As a result, the robot may persist along its current heading even when initially oriented away from the goal, moving toward directions that do not decrease the global topological distance. In this work, we propose IntentReact, an intent-conditioned object-centric navigation framework that introduces a compact interface between global topological planning and reactive object-centric control. Our approach encodes global topological guidance as a low-dimensional directional signal, termed intent, which conditions a learned waypoint prediction policy to bias navigation toward topologically consistent progression. This design enables the robot to promptly reorient when local observations are misleading, guiding motion toward directions that decrease global topological distance while preserving the reactivity and robustness of object-centric control. We evaluate the proposed framework through extensive experiments, demonstrating improved navigation success and execution quality compared to prior object-centric navigation methods.

</details>


### [177] [UMBRELLA: Uncertainty-aware Multi-robot Reactive Coordination under Dynamic Temporal Logic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25395)
*Qisheng Zhao,Meng Guo,Hengxuan Du,Lars Lindemann,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UMBRELLA框架，用于协调多机器人系统完成涉及动态移动目标的协作任务，通过结合蒙特卡洛树搜索和不确定性感知的rollout，最小化平均完工时间的条件风险价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人协调方法通常假设静态任务特征或简单重规划，难以处理动态移动目标。本文旨在解决多机器人系统在动态不确定环境中协调协作任务的挑战性问题。

Method: 提出UMBRELLA框架：1) 使用Conformal Prediction建模目标运动预测的不确定性；2) 结合蒙特卡洛树搜索与不确定性感知rollout；3) 引入CP-based度量指导加速搜索；4) 采用滚动时域规划方案动态调整任务分配；5) 确保任务间的时空约束，仅需在线执行时的部分同步。

Result: 大规模仿真和硬件实验表明，相比静态基线方法，平均完工时间减少23%，方差减少71%，显著提升了多机器人系统在动态环境中的协调效率。

Conclusion: UMBRELLA框架有效解决了多机器人系统在动态不确定环境中协调协作任务的问题，通过不确定性建模和滚动时域规划，显著提升了系统性能，为动态目标的多机器人协调提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can be extremely efficient for accomplishing team-wise tasks by acting concurrently and collaboratively. However, most existing methods either assume static task features or simply replan when environmental changes occur. This paper addresses the challenging problem of coordinating multi-robot systems for collaborative tasks involving dynamic and moving targets. We explicitly model the uncertainty in target motion prediction via Conformal Prediction(CP), while respecting the spatial-temporal constraints specified by Linear Temporal Logic (LTL). The proposed framework (UMBRELLA) combines the Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over partial plans with uncertainty-aware rollouts, and introduces a CP-based metric to guide and accelerate the search. The objective is to minimize the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) of the average makespan. For tasks released online, a receding-horizon planning scheme dynamically adjusts the assignments based on updated task specifications and motion predictions. Spatial and temporal constraints among the tasks are always ensured, and only partial synchronization is required for the collaborative tasks during online execution. Extensive large-scale simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate substantial reductions in both the average makespan and its variance by 23% and 71%, compared with static baselines.

</details>


### [178] [System Design for Maintaining Internal State Consistency in Long-Horizon Robotic Tabletop Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25405)
*Guangyu Zhao,Ceyao Zhang,Chengdong Ma,Tao Wu,Yiyang Song,Haoxuan Ru,Yifan Zhong,Ruilin Yan,Lingfeng Li,Ruochong Li,Yu Li,Xuyuan Han,Yun Ding,Ruizhang Jiang,Xiaochuan Zhang,Yichao Li,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang,Yitao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于长时程桌面游戏（以麻将为例）的机器人系统架构，通过显式状态管理、模块分区和恢复机制来维持内部状态一致性，避免错误传播导致任务失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程桌面游戏对机器人系统提出了独特挑战：微小的感知或执行错误会破坏累积的任务状态，在决策模块间传播，最终导致交互失败。需要系统级设计而非孤立组件改进来维持内部状态一致性。

Method: 提出集成架构：显式维护感知、执行和交互状态；将高层语义推理与时间关键感知控制分离；采用带触觉触发恢复的已验证动作基元防止状态损坏；引入交互级监控机制检测回合违规和隐藏信息泄露。

Result: 实现了完整游戏操作，并对故障模式、恢复效果、跨模块错误传播以及硬件算法权衡进行了实证分析。结果显示显式分区、监控状态转换和恢复机制对维持长时程可执行一致性至关重要。

Conclusion: 显式分区、监控状态转换和恢复机制是维持长时程可执行一致性的关键，而单体或未验证的流水线会导致端到端可靠性显著下降。该系统为研究长时程回合制交互中的系统级设计原则提供了实证平台。

Abstract: Long-horizon tabletop games pose a distinct systems challenge for robotics: small perceptual or execution errors can invalidate accumulated task state, propagate across decision-making modules, and ultimately derail interaction. This paper studies how to maintain internal state consistency in turn-based, multi-human robotic tabletop games through deliberate system design rather than isolated component improvement. Using Mahjong as a representative long-horizon setting, we present an integrated architecture that explicitly maintains perceptual, execution, and interaction state, partitions high-level semantic reasoning from time-critical perception and control, and incorporates verified action primitives with tactile-triggered recovery to prevent premature state corruption. We further introduce interaction-level monitoring mechanisms to detect turn violations and hidden-information breaches that threaten execution assumptions. Beyond demonstrating complete-game operation, we provide an empirical characterization of failure modes, recovery effectiveness, cross-module error propagation, and hardware-algorithm trade-offs observed during deployment. Our results show that explicit partitioning, monitored state transitions, and recovery mechanisms are critical for sustaining executable consistency over extended play, whereas monolithic or unverified pipelines lead to measurable degradation in end-to-end reliability. The proposed system serves as an empirical platform for studying system-level design principles in long-horizon, turn-based interaction.

</details>


### [179] [MMaDA-VLA: Large Diffusion Vision-Language-Action Model with Unified Multi-Modal Instruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25406)
*Yang Liu,Pengxiang Ding,Tengyue Jiang,Xudong Wang,Wenxuan Song,Minghui Lin,Han Zhao,Hongyin Zhang,Zifeng Zhuang,Wei Zhao,Siteng Huang,Jinkui Shi,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MMaDA-VLA是一个原生扩散视觉-语言-动作模型，通过离散扩散统一多模态理解和生成，在单个框架中并行生成未来目标观测和动作块，无需额外世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型存在架构冗余、时序不一致、长视野误差累积问题，且缺乏捕捉环境动态的机制，需要额外模块。

Method: 提出原生离散扩散公式，将语言、图像和连续机器人控制嵌入统一离散标记空间，通过掩码标记去噪训练单一骨干网络，并行生成未来目标观测和动作块。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实任务中达到SOTA性能：LIBERO上98.0%平均成功率，CALVIN上4.78平均长度。

Conclusion: MMaDA-VLA通过统一扩散框架解决了现有VLA模型的局限性，实现了高效的多模态理解和生成，在机器人控制任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim to control robots for manipulation from visual observations and natural-language instructions. However, existing hierarchical and autoregressive paradigms often introduce architectural overhead, suffer from temporal inconsistency and long-horizon error accumulation, and lack a mechanism to capture environment dynamics without extra modules. To this end, we present MMaDA-VLA, a fully native pre-trained large diffusion VLA model that unifies multi-modal understanding and generation in a single framework. Our key idea is a native discrete diffusion formulation that embeds language, images, and continuous robot controls into one discrete token space and trains a single backbone with masked token denoising to jointly generate a future goal observation and an action chunk in parallel. Iterative denoising enables global, order-free refinement, improving long-horizon consistency while grounding actions in predicted future visual outcomes without auxiliary world models. Experiments across simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show state-of-the-art performance, achieving 98.0% average success on LIBERO and 4.78 average length on CALVIN.

</details>


### [180] [Visualizing Impedance Control in Augmented Reality for Teleoperation: Design and User Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25418)
*Gijs van den Brandt,Femke van Beek,Elena Torta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用AR可视化阻抗控制器目标位姿及其与机器人末端位移，为无触觉反馈的运动控制提供力觉感知，提升接触丰富的遥操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本、仅有运动控制的遥操作系统缺乏触觉反馈，操作者无法感知和调节接触力，限制了接触丰富操作任务的性能。虚拟现实控制器虽然提供直观运动控制，但无法直接感知力反馈。

Method: 提出增强现实(AR)可视化阻抗控制器的目标位姿及其与机器人末端执行器的位移差异。这种可视化传达了控制器产生的力，为操作者提供直观的实时力反馈，无需昂贵的触觉硬件。

Result: 在17名参与者的双臂操作研究中，AR可视化将力关键提升任务的完成时间减少了24%，但对于滑动任务（精确力控制不太关键）没有显著影响。

Conclusion: 通过AR使阻抗目标可见是改善接触丰富遥操作中人机交互的可行方法，为低成本遥操作系统提供了有效的力反馈替代方案。

Abstract: Teleoperation for contact-rich manipulation remains challenging, especially when using low-cost, motion-only interfaces that provide no haptic feedback. Virtual reality controllers enable intuitive motion control but do not allow operators to directly perceive or regulate contact forces, limiting task performance. To address this, we propose an augmented reality (AR) visualization of the impedance controller's target pose and its displacement from each robot end effector. This visualization conveys the forces generated by the controller, providing operators with intuitive, real-time feedback without expensive haptic hardware. We evaluate the design in a dual-arm manipulation study with 17 participants who repeatedly reposition a box with and without the AR visualization. Results show that AR visualization reduces completion time by 24% for force-critical lifting tasks, with no significant effect on sliding tasks where precise force control is less critical. These findings indicate that making the impedance target visible through AR is a viable approach to improve human-robot interaction for contact-rich teleoperation.

</details>


### [181] [Temporally Decoupled Diffusion Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25462)
*Xiang Li,Bikun Wang,John Zhang,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TDDM提出了一种时间解耦的扩散模型，通过噪声掩码范式将轨迹分割为不同噪声水平的片段，分别处理短期动态约束和长期导航目标，在nuPlan基准测试中达到或超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将轨迹视为单一实体，忽略了异质的时间依赖性：近期规划受瞬时动态约束，远期规划受导航目标约束。需要平衡即时安全与长期目标。

Method: 提出时间解耦扩散模型(TDDM)：1) 噪声掩码范式将轨迹分割为独立噪声水平的片段；2) 引入时间解耦自适应层归一化(TD-AdaLN)注入片段特定时间步；3) 推理时使用非对称时间分类器自由引导，利用弱噪声远期先验指导近期路径生成。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，TDDM达到或超越现有最佳基线，在具有挑战性的Test14-hard子集表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: TDDM通过时间解耦方法有效处理动态城市环境中轨迹规划的异质时间依赖性，在安全性和目标达成之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic urban environments requires balancing immediate safety with long-term goals. While diffusion models effectively capture multi-modal decision-making, existing approaches treat trajectories as monolithic entities, overlooking heterogeneous temporal dependencies where near-term plans are constrained by instantaneous dynamics and far-term plans by navigational goals. To address this, we propose Temporally Decoupled Diffusion Model (TDDM), which reformulates trajectory generation via a noise-as-mask paradigm. By partitioning trajectories into segments with independent noise levels, we implicitly treat high noise as information voids and weak noise as contextual cues. This compels the model to reconstruct corrupted near-term states by leveraging internal correlations with better-preserved temporal contexts. Architecturally, we introduce a Temporally Decoupled Adaptive Layer Normalization (TD-AdaLN) to inject segment-specific timesteps. During inference, our Asymmetric Temporal Classifier-Free Guidance utilizes weakly noised far-term priors to guide immediate path generation. Evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark show TDDM approaches or exceeds state-of-the-art baselines, particularly excelling in the challenging Test14-hard subset.

</details>


### [182] [LILAC: Language-Conditioned Object-Centric Optical Flow for Open-Loop Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25481)
*Motonari Kambara,Koki Seno,Tomoya Kaichi,Yanan Wang,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LILAC：基于光流的语言指令引导开环动作生成器，通过生成物体中心的光流并将其转换为6自由度机械臂轨迹，实现语言条件机器人操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语言条件机器人操作中从预操作图像和自然语言指令生成物体轨迹的挑战，需要适当的指令-光流对齐，同时减少对大量机器人数据的需求

Method: 提出LILAC（基于语言指令的开环动作生成器），包含两个关键组件：语义对齐损失（增强语言条件以生成指令对齐的光流）和提示条件跨模态适配器（对齐学习的视觉提示与图像和文本特征）

Result: 在多个基准测试中，该方法在生成光流质量方面优于现有方法；在物理物体操作实验中，使用自由形式指令时，LILAC展示了比现有方法更优的任务成功率

Conclusion: LILAC通过流基轨迹生成实现了高效的语言条件机器人操作，仅需少量本体特定数据，在光流质量和实际操作性能方面均表现出色

Abstract: We address language-conditioned robotic manipulation using flow-based trajectory generation, which enables training on human and web videos of object manipulation and requires only minimal embodiment-specific data. This task is challenging, as object trajectory generation from pre-manipulation images and natural language instructions requires appropriate instruction-flow alignment. To tackle this challenge, we propose the flow-based Language Instruction-guided open-Loop ACtion generator (LILAC). This flow-based Vision-Language-Action model (VLA) generates object-centric 2D optical flow from an RGB image and a natural language instruction, and converts the flow into a 6-DoF manipulator trajectory. LILAC incorporates two key components: Semantic Alignment Loss, which strengthens language conditioning to generate instruction-aligned optical flow, and Prompt-Conditioned Cross-Modal Adapter, which aligns learned visual prompts with image and text features to provide rich cues for flow generation. Experimentally, our method outperformed existing approaches in generated flow quality across multiple benchmarks. Furthermore, in physical object manipulation experiments using free-form instructions, LILAC demonstrated a superior task success rate compared to existing methods. The project page is available at https://lilac-75srg.kinsta.page/.

</details>


### [183] [Towards Embodied AI with MuscleMimic: Unlocking full-body musculoskeletal motor learning at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25544)
*Chengkun Li,Cheryl Wang,Bianca Ziliotto,Merkourios Simos,Jozsef Kovecses,Guillaume Durandau,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuscleMimic是一个开源框架，用于在生理真实、肌肉驱动的人体模型上进行可扩展的运动模仿学习，提供验证过的肌肉骨骼模型和GPU加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉驱动肌肉骨骼模型的运动控制学习受到计算成本高和缺乏验证的开放全身模型的限制，需要降低计算和数据障碍。

Method: 提供两个验证的肌肉骨骼模型（上半身126肌肉，全身416肌肉），SMPL格式运动捕捉数据重定向管道，利用大规模并行GPU模拟加速训练，保持全面碰撞处理。

Result: 相比之前CPU方法实现数量级训练加速，单一通用策略可在数天内训练数百种不同运动，策略能忠实再现人类运动，微调新运动仅需数小时，生物力学验证显示关节运动学高度一致（平均相关性r=0.90）。

Conclusion: MuscleMimic通过降低肌肉骨骼模拟的计算和数据障碍，实现了跨不同动态运动的系统模型验证，促进了神经肌肉控制研究的广泛参与。

Abstract: Learning motor control for muscle-driven musculoskeletal models is hindered by the computational cost of biomechanically accurate simulation and the scarcity of validated, open full-body models. Here we present MuscleMimic, an open-source framework for scalable motion imitation learning with physiologically realistic, muscle-actuated humanoids. MuscleMimic provides two validated musculoskeletal embodiments - a fixed-root upper-body model (126 muscles) for bimanual manipulation and a full-body model (416 muscles) for locomotion - together with a retargeting pipeline that maps SMPL-format motion capture data onto musculoskeletal structures while preserving kinematic and dynamic consistency. Leveraging massively parallel GPU simulation, the framework achieves order-of-magnitude training speedups over prior CPU-based approaches while maintaining comprehensive collision handling, enabling a single generalist policy to be trained on hundreds of diverse motions within days. The resulting policy faithfully reproduces a broad repertoire of human movements under full muscular control and can be fine-tuned to novel motions within hours. Biomechanical validation against experimental walking and running data demonstrates strong agreement in joint kinematics (mean correlation r = 0.90), while muscle activation analysis reveals both the promise and fundamental challenges of achieving physiological fidelity through kinematic imitation alone. By lowering the computational and data barriers to musculoskeletal simulation, MuscleMimic enables systematic model validation across diverse dynamic movements and broader participation in neuromuscular control research. Code, models, checkpoints, and retargeted datasets are available at: https://github.com/amathislab/musclemimic

</details>


### [184] [Towards Generalizable Robotic Data Flywheel: High-Dimensional Factorization and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25583)
*Yuyang Xiao,Yifei Zhou,Haoran Wang,Wenxuan Ou,Yuxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: F-ACIL是一个启发式因子感知的组合迭代学习框架，通过结构化数据分解和组合泛化来解决机器人学习中的数据多样性和效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通用模型面临的主要瓶颈是缺乏足够多样化的数据以及数据效率有限，而系统性的数据收集和整理策略尚未充分探索。任务多样性源于多个维度上稀疏分布的隐含因素，这些因素难以明确定义。

Method: 提出F-ACIL框架，将数据分布分解为结构化因子空间（如物体、动作、环境），基于因子化公式开发因子级数据收集和迭代训练范式，促进高维因子空间上的组合泛化。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，F-ACIL相比无策略方法，使用5-10倍更少的演示数据实现了超过45%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化因子分解为真实世界机器人学习中的高效组合泛化提供了实用途径，F-ACIL可以启发更多关于构建可泛化机器人数据飞轮策略的系统性研究。

Abstract: The lack of sufficiently diverse data, coupled with limited data efficiency, remains a major bottleneck for generalist robotic models, yet systematic strategies for collecting and curating such data are not fully explored. Task diversity arises from implicit factors that are sparsely distributed across multiple dimensions and are difficult to define explicitly. To address this challenge, we propose F-ACIL, a heuristic factor-aware compositional iterative learning framework that enables structured data factorization and promotes compositional generalization. F-ACIL decomposes the data distribution into structured factor spaces such as object, action, and environment. Based on the factorized formulation, we develop a factor-wise data collection and an iterative training paradigm that promotes compositional generalization over the high-dimensional factor space, leading to more effective utilization of real-world robotic demonstrations. With extensive real-world experiments, we show that F-ACIL can achieve more than 45% performance gains with 5-10$\times$ fewer demonstrations comparing to that of which without the strategy. The results suggest that structured factorization offers a practical pathway toward efficient compositional generalization in real-world robotic learning. We believe F-ACIL can inspire more systematic research on building generalizable robotic data flywheel strategies. More demonstrations can be found at: https://f-acil.github.io/

</details>


### [185] [Accurate Surface and Reflectance Modelling from 3D Radar Data with Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25623)
*Judith Treffler,Vladimír Kubelka,Henrik Andreasson,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经隐式表示的雷达点云3D重建方法，联合建模场景几何和雷达反射强度，在低能见度环境下优于传统方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达在低能见度环境（雾、烟、灰尘）中比相机和激光雷达更具优势，但雷达数据稀疏且噪声大，传统3D表面重建方法效果不佳

Method: 采用神经隐式方法，通过内存高效的混合特征编码学习连续有符号距离场（SDF）进行表面重建，同时捕获雷达特有的反射特性

Result: 相比现有激光雷达重建方法应用于雷达数据，该方法产生更平滑、更准确的3D表面重建，并能重建视角相关的雷达强度；在输入点云更稀疏时，神经隐式表示比传统显式SDF和网格化技术重建更准确

Conclusion: 神经隐式表示方法能有效处理稀疏噪声雷达数据，在低能见度环境下实现可靠的3D场景重建，为自动驾驶系统提供鲁棒的场景表示

Abstract: Robust scene representation is essential for autonomous systems to safely operate in challenging low-visibility environments. Radar has a clear advantage over cameras and lidars in these conditions due to its resilience to environmental factors such as fog, smoke, or dust. However, radar data is inherently sparse and noisy, making reliable 3D surface reconstruction challenging. To address these challenges, we propose a neural implicit approach for 3D mapping from radar point clouds, which jointly models scene geometry and view-dependent radar intensities. Our method leverages a memory-efficient hybrid feature encoding to learn a continuous Signed Distance Field (SDF) for surface reconstruction, while also capturing radar-specific reflective properties. We show that our approach produces smoother, more accurate 3D surface reconstructions compared to existing lidar-based reconstruction methods applied to radar data, and can reconstruct view-dependent radar intensities. We also show that in general, as input point clouds get sparser, neural implicit representations render more faithful surfaces, compared to traditional explicit SDFs and meshing techniques.

</details>


### [186] [A Mentalistic Interface for Probing Folk-Psychological Attribution to Non-Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25646)
*Giulio Pisaneschi,Pierpaolo Serio,Estelle Gerbier,Andrea Dan Ryals,Lorenzo Pollini,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个实验平台，用于研究人们对非人形机器人意向状态归因，通过保持行为不变但改变解释框架（心理主义、目的论、机械论）来探究语言和框架如何影响意向立场的采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言和解释框架如何影响人们对机器人意向状态的归因，特别是在非人形机器人情境下，理解意向立场在机器人学中的形成机制。

Method: 开发了一个结合模拟机器人、真实任务环境和基于大语言模型的解释层的实验平台，能够用心理主义、目的论和机械论三种不同术语表达相同行为。

Result: 该平台提供了一个受控的方法，能够在保持机器人行为不变的情况下，通过改变解释框架来研究意向立场的形成过程。

Conclusion: 这个实验平台为研究语言和框架如何塑造机器人学中的意向立场采纳提供了有价值的工具，有助于理解人们对非人形机器人意向状态归因的心理机制。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental platform for studying intentional-state attribution toward a non-humanoid robot. The system combines a simulated robot, realistic task environments, and large language model-based explanatory layers that can express the same behavior in mentalistic, teleological, or mechanistic terms. By holding behavior constant while varying the explanatory frame, the platform provides a controlled way to investigate how language and framing shape the adoption of the intentional stance in robotics.

</details>


### [187] [Fast-dVLA: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion VLA to Real-Time Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25661)
*Wenxuan Song,Jiayi Chen,Shuai Chen,Jingbo Wang,Pengxiang Ding,Han Zhao,Yikai Qin,Xinhu Zheng,Donglin Wang,Yan Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fast-dVLA方法，通过解耦辅助任务训练的两个目标（增强通用能力和拟合任务特定动作分布），在小型任务集上训练得到能力向量，与预训练参数合并形成能力增强的元模型，结合轻量正交正则化损失，在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的VLA模型在标准监督微调中往往无法有效提升性能并降低适应成本。现有辅助训练方法虽然能提升性能并减少收敛步数，但会因额外的辅助任务损失带来显著计算开销。需要同时实现辅助训练的能力增强效果和标准SFT的简洁性。

Method: 1. 在参数空间中解耦辅助任务训练的两个目标：增强通用能力和拟合任务特定动作分布。2. 在小型任务集上使用两种不同训练策略训练模型收敛。3. 将得到的模型参数差异解释为辅助任务提供的能力向量。4. 将这些向量与预训练参数合并形成能力增强的元模型。5. 在标准SFT中增加轻量正交正则化损失。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在多种机器人任务中非常有效，能够在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: Fast-dVLA方法成功解决了预训练VLA模型在标准SFT中性能提升有限和适应成本高的问题，通过解耦训练目标、提取能力向量并与预训练参数合并，实现了计算效率与性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel approach to address the challenge that pretrained VLA models often fail to effectively improve performance and reduce adaptation costs during standard supervised finetuning (SFT). Some advanced finetuning methods with auxiliary training objectives can improve performance and reduce the number of convergence steps. However, they typically incur significant computational overhead due to the additional losses from auxiliary tasks. To simultaneously achieve the enhanced capabilities of auxiliary training with the simplicity of standard SFT, we decouple the two objectives of auxiliary task training within the parameter space, namely, enhancing general capabilities and fitting task-specific action distributions. To deliver this goal, we only need to train the model to converge on a small-scale task set using two distinct training strategies. The difference between the resulting model parameters can then be interpreted as capability vectors provided by auxiliary tasks. These vectors are then merged with pretrained parameters to form a capability-enhanced meta model. Moreover, when standard SFT is augmented with a lightweight orthogonal regularization loss, the merged model attains performance comparable to auxiliary finetuned baselines with reduced computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach is highly effective across diverse robot tasks. Project page: https://chris1220313648.github.io/Fast-dVLA/

</details>


### [188] [Can Users Specify Driving Speed? Bench2Drive-Speed: Benchmark and Baselines for Desired-Speed Conditioned Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25672)
*Yuqian Shao,Xiaosong Jia,Langechuan Liu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Bench2Drive-Speed基准，用于评估自动驾驶策略如何遵循用户指定的速度要求和超车指令，通过重新标注现有驾驶数据实现速度监督，无需额外数据收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶研究忽视了用户自定义速度需求和超车指令的功能，需要建立能够响应这些用户指令的自动驾驶系统。
